=== SURVIVABILITY GAP ANALYSIS ===

but not with any goodput-free action (Map A: non-recoverable): survival bought

none                          0.840      0.00016     100%            N/A            N/A

Mean DQN latency:  0.892 ms
Mean Rule latency: 0.00012 ms
Speed ratio: Rule-Based is ~7151x faster than DQN (lookup table vs neural net inference)
DQN-vs-Rule agreement (overall, all threats x SNR): 59.3%

NOTE: both policies act through the same physical countermeasure model
(apply_countermeasure.m, D28), so agreement and recovery compare decision
quality only. Disagreement is no longer cosmetic: actions differ in effect.

Interpretation: the rule is effectively instant (a lookup); the DQN costs about a
millisecond of inference, small next to the ~10 ms detection path. Both are far
faster than the dwell of a few frames, so recovery time is set by detection and
hysteresis rather than by the policy's compute time (section 5).
